# Image Generation Prompts for Driving Test Questions

**Rules that apply to every prompt below (the prompts for questions that still need an image repeat them):**
- The setting is Japan with **left-hand traffic**: vehicles keep to the left side of the road and cars are right-hand drive (steering wheel on the right side). Intersections, turns, overtaking and crossings must follow that.
- Each of those prompts ends with an exact **Mongolian Cyrillic caption** to render on a banner at the bottom of the image. Copy the letters exactly as quoted; do not translate, rephrase or add other text.
- Images that already exist on S3 were made before these rules and are not regenerated.

These prompts are designed to be used with AI image generators (like Midjourney, DALL-E 3, or Stable Diffusion). They use keywords like "flat vector illustration," "educational style," and "driving manual" to ensure a consistent, easy-to-understand visual style suitable for a test interface.

## Item 1: Driver's attitude and manners (運転者の心得)

*   **1-1:** Flat vector illustration of two cars at an intersection, one driver smiling and politely gesturing with their hand to let the other car pass, sunny daylight, educational driving manual style --ar 16:9
*   **1-2:** Warning illustration of a moving car on a road, a driver's arm reaching out the window throwing a cigarette butt and an empty can, red danger symbols, flat vector --ar 16:9
*   **1-3:** Illustration of a car driving quietly through a residential neighborhood, people relaxing in their yards, peaceful atmosphere, driving etiquette concept --ar 16:9
*   **1-4:** Educational diagram showing a baby in a car child seat installed incorrectly vs correctly, instructional manual style, flat vector --ar 16:9
*   **1-5:** Illustration of a car stopping at a crosswalk to let a pedestrian pass, even though the pedestrian is crossing outside the lines, safety first concept --ar 16:9
*   **1-6:** Flat vector illustration of a confusing city street with neon signs that look exactly like red and green traffic lights right next to the actual traffic signal, warning concept --ar 16:9
*   **1-7:** Diagram showing a driver's leg completely straight and locked while pressing the brake pedal, with a big red X indicating incorrect seating posture, side view --ar 16:9
*   **1-8:** Diagram showing a driver sitting in a car with hands on the steering wheel, elbows slightly bent at a comfortable angle, green checkmark for correct posture, side view --ar 16:9
*   **1-9:** Illustration of a driver leaning heavily to the side with their elbow resting on the car window frame looking tired, big red X for bad driving posture --ar 16:9
*   **1-10:** Warning illustration of a driver behind the steering wheel with a slight blush on their cheeks and a beer bottle nearby, big red X indicating no drunk driving, flat vector --ar 16:9
*   **1-11:** Infographic illustration showing a car crash connecting to three icons: a police badge (criminal), money/gavel (civil), and a revoked license (administrative), educational style --ar 16:9
*   **1-12:** Illustration of a small toddler sitting in the back seat of a car wearing an adult seat belt that doesn't fit properly, big red X indicating danger --ar 16:9
*   **1-13:** Illustration of a happy toddler safely strapped into a properly fitted child seat in the back seat of a car, green checkmark for safety --ar 16:9
*   **1-14:** Illustration of a motorcycle rider in summer wearing full protective gear, long sleeves, pants, and a helmet, flat vector educational style --ar 16:9
*   **1-15:** Illustration of a motorcycle rider wearing a yellow plastic construction hard-hat instead of a proper motorcycle helmet, big red X, warning style --ar 16:9
*   **1-16:** Illustration of a parent and child playing soccer on the side of a busy road with cars passing by, dangerous situation, big red X --ar 16:9
*   **1-17:** Illustration comparing two pairs of feet on car pedals: one wearing safe flat driving shoes (green checkmark), the other wearing high heels or thick sandals (red X) --ar 16:9
*   **1-18:** Flat vector illustration of a driver holding up a valid Driver's License card (or My Number card) before getting into their car --ar 16:9
*   **1-19:** Illustration of a construction worker removing an official road sign and throwing it away, with a police officer looking angry, big red X --ar 16:9
*   **1-20:** Interior view of a car with a deployed airbag, showing a driver not wearing a seat belt getting injured, warning illustration against relying only on airbags --ar 16:9
*   **1-21:** Close-up diagram of a person wearing a seat belt, highlighting the lap belt positioned low and tight across the pelvic bones, green checkmark --ar 16:9
*   **1-22:** Interior illustration of a car looking from the front, showing the driver and all rear-seat passengers wearing their seat belts properly --ar 16:9
*   **1-23:** Illustration of a pregnant woman or a person with bandages in a car, exempt from wearing a seat belt due to medical reasons, educational style --ar 16:9
*   **1-24:** Illustration of a driver looking down at their glowing smartphone screen while driving, dangerous driving concept, big red X --ar 16:9
*   **1-25:** Illustration of a driver putting their smartphone into the glove box or pressing a "Drive Mode" button before turning the steering wheel, green checkmark --ar 16:9
*   **1-26:** Illustration of a drunk person sleeping on the asphalt in the middle of a road with a car approaching, high danger warning illustration --ar 16:9
*   **1-27:** Illustration of a road with a designated sidewalk, but the sidewalk is completely blocked by potted plants, forcing pedestrians onto the road, big red X --ar 16:9
*   **1-28:** Illustration of a driver staring intensely at the car's navigation screen while driving, completely ignoring the road ahead, red X warning --ar 16:9
*   **1-29:** Illustration of a person putting their car's vehicle inspection certificate and insurance papers into a drawer inside their house, red X (incorrect location) --ar 16:9
*   **1-30:** Illustration of a driver opening the car's glove compartment to show the vehicle inspection certificate and insurance papers stored safely inside, green checkmark --ar 16:9
